
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- RAG-CDB ---
how many of the patients had hemorrhage complicating a procedure?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Hemorrhage complicating a procedure" OR DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Hemorrhage complic proc"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'hemorrhage complicating a procedure or diagnoses. short_title = hemorrhage complic proc']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Hemorrhage complicating a procedure"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'hemorrhage complicating a procedure']]}
----------

Calulate the total number of patients who had icd9 code 5771
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "5771"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', '5771']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE PROCEDURES."ICD9_CODE" = "5771"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."icd9_code"', '=', '5771']]}
----------

how many patients use the neonatal syringe?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT PRESCRIPTIONS."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM PRESCRIPTIONS 
WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Syringe (Neonatal)"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['prescriptions."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'syringe (neonatal)']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Syringe (Neonatal)"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'syringe (neonatal)']]}
----------

PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "portal hypertension"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'portal hypertension']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "SINGLE" AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Portal hypertension"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'single'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'portal hypertension']]}

find the number of russian speaking patients who are diagnosed with proteus infection nos.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID")
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC
JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = DIAGNOSES."SUBJECT_ID" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."HADM_ID" = DIAGNOSES."HADM_ID"
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."LANGUAGE" = 'RUSS' AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = 'Proteus infection, nos'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."language"', '=', 'russ'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'proteus infection, nos']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."LANGUAGE" = "RUSS" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Proteus infection NOS"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."language"', '=', 'russ'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'proteus infection nos']]}
----------

Look for the number of elective hospital admission patients who had a unilateral radical neck dissection procedure.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN PROCEDURES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "ELECTIVE" 
AND PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" = "Radical neck dissection, unilateral"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'elective'], ['procedures."long_title"', '=', 'radical neck dissection, unilateral']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "ELECTIVE" AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Unilat rad neck dissect"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'elective'], ['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'unilat rad neck dissect']]}
----------

Calculate the number of patients admitted to emergency who had an ld body fluid test by lab
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN LAB ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = 'EMERGENCY' 
AND LAB."FLUID" = 'LD, Body Fluid'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'ld, body fluid']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND LAB."LABEL" = "LD, Body Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'ld, body fluid']]}
----------

give the number of patients whose death status is 0 and diagnosis short title is ac diastolic heart failure.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = 0 
AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Ac diastolic hrt fail"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'ac diastolic hrt fail']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Ac diastolic hrt failure"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'ac diastolic hrt failure']]}
----------

provide the number of patients with a diagnoses of personal history of other malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction and anus and are still alive.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Personal history of malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction, and anus" 
AND DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = 0
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'personal history of malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction,']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Hx-rectal & anal malign"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'hx-rectal & anal malign']]}
----------

----------
PREDS:

How many patients have gastrointestinal bleed and tp drug route?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Gastrointestinal bleed" 
AND PRESCRIPTIONS."ROUTE" = "TP"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'gastrointestinal bleed'], ['prescriptions."route"', '=', 'tp']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "GASTROINTESTINAL BLEED" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."ROUTE" = "TP"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'gastrointestinal bleed'], ['prescriptions."route"', '=', 'tp']]}
----------

Get the number of patients who died in or before 2132 and were less than 72 years of age.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < 72 AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= 2132.0 AND DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = 1
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '72'], ['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2132.0'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '1']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "72" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2132.0"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '72'], ['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2132.0']]}
----------

how many patients are below 44 years of age and followed the procedure uni salpingo-oophor?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN PROCEDURES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < 44 
AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Uni salpingo-oophor"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '44'], ['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'uni salpingo-oophor']]}
----------
TRUTH:

----------
PREDS:
SELECT DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID"

INNER JOIN LAB ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Dysphagia NOS" 
AND LAB."LABEL" LIKE "%ketone%"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'dysphagia nos'], ['lab."label"', 'like', '%ketone%']]}

count the number of patients who have stayed in the hospital for more than 23 days with a drug phenylephrine hcl.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" > 23 
AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = 'Phenylephrine HCl'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."days_stay"', '>', '23'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenylephrine hcl']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_DOSE" FROM PRESCRIPTIONS WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Phenytoin"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions."drug_dose"'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenytoin']]}
----------

calculate the total number of unmarried patients. 
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" != "MARRIED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" != "WIDOWED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" != "DIVORCED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" != "SEPARATED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" IS NOT NULL;
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '!=', 'divorced'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '!=', 'married'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '!=', 'separated'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '!=', 'widowed'], ['demographic."marital_status"', 'is', 'not null;']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "SINGLE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'single']]}
----------

Which patients had a csf glucose lab test done?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN LAB ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE LAB."LABEL" LIKE '%glucose%' AND LAB."FLUID" = "Cerebrospinal Fluid (CSF)"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['lab."fluid"', '=', 'cerebrospinal fluid (csf)'], ['lab."label"', 'like', '%glucose%']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE LAB."LABEL" = "Glucose, CSF"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['lab."label"', '=', 'glucose, csf']]}
----------

find the male patients who have diagnoses icd9 code 5119.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."GENDER" = "M" AND DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "5119"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."gender"', '=', 'm'], ['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', '5119']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."GENDER" = "M" AND DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "5119"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."gender"', '=', 'm'], ['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', '5119']]}

how many patients with white ethnic background are diagnosed with unspecified pleural effusion?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ETHNICITY" = "WHITE" 
AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Unspecified pleural effusion"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."ethnicity"', '=', 'white'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'unspecified pleural effusion']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ETHNICITY" = "WHITE" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Pleural effusion NOS"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."ethnicity"', '=', 'white'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'pleural effusion nos']]}

----------
PREDS:

WHERE DIAGNOSIS = 'GUILLAIN BARRE SYNDROME' 
AND DAYS_STAY > 10
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['days_stay', '>', '10'], ['diagnosis', '=', 'guillain barre syndrome']]}

provide the number of patients whose diagnosis short title is dmi keto nt st uncontrolled and lab test category is blood gas.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
INNER JOIN LAB ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "DMI keto nt st uncontrolled" 
AND LAB."CATEGORY" = "Blood Gas"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'dmi keto nt st uncontrolled'], ['lab."category"', '=', 'blood gas']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "DMI keto nt st uncntrld" AND LAB."CATEGORY" = "Blood Gas"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'dmi keto nt st uncntrld'], ['lab."category"', '=', 'blood gas']]}

Calculate the total number of patients who had transient ischemic attack as their primary disease.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT SUBJECT_ID) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DIAGNOSIS = 'TRANSIENT ISCHEMIC ATTACK'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['diagnosis', '=', 'transient ischemic attack']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "TRANSIENT ISCHEMIC ATTACK"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'transient ischemic attack']]}
----------

tell me the number of patients who were born in or before 2132.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DOB_YEAR" <= "2132"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."dob_year"', '<=', '2132']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2132.0"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2132.0']]}
----------

how many patients have been diagnosed with unspecified pseudomonas infection?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Unspecified pseudomonas infection"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'unspecified pseudomonas infection']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Pseudomonas infect NOS"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'pseudomonas infect nos']]}
----------

how many of the unmarried patients belonged to white ethnic origin?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" != "MARRIED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."ETHNICITY" = "WHITE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."ethnicity"', '=', 'white'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '!=', 'married']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "SINGLE" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."ETHNICITY" = "WHITE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."ethnicity"', '=', 'white'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'single']]}
----------

provide the number of divorced patients who were diagnosed with occlusion and stenosis of carotid artery without mention of cerebral infarction.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = 'DIVORCED' 
AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = 'Occlusion and stenosis of carotid artery without mention of cerebral infarction'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'divorced'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'occlusion'], ['stenosis', 'of', 'carotid artery without mention of cerebral infarction']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "DIVORCED" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Ocl crtd art wo infrct"

find the number of urgent hospital admission patients who have acute salpingitis and oophoritis diagnoses.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC."HADM_ID" = DIAGNOSES."HADM_ID" 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = 'URGENT' 
AND (DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" LIKE '%acute salpingitis%' OR DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" LIKE '%oophoritis%')
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['(diagnoses."long_title"', 'like', '%acute salpingitis% or diagnoses. long_title like %oophoritis% )'], ['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'urgent']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "URGENT" AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Acute salpingitis and oophoritis"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'urgent'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'acute salpingitis']]}

how many patients are diagnosed with icd9 code 9600 have an additive drug type?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "9600" 
AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_TYPE" = "ADDITIVE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', '9600'], ['prescriptions."drug_type"', '=', 'additive']]}

----------
PREDS:

WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC.ETHNICITY = 'WHITE - RUSSIAN' 
AND PRESCRIPTIONS.ROUTE = 'NEB'
{'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic.ethnicity', '=', 'white - russian'], ['prescriptions.route', '=', 'neb']]}

calculate the number of patients diagnosed with icd9 code 29570 whose death status is unspecified
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" IS NULL 
AND DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "29570"

how many patients are diagnosed with the history of tobacco use and tested under the lab category blood gas?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
INNER JOIN LAB ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES.LONG_TITLE LIKE '%history of tobacco use%' 
AND LAB.CATEGORY = 'Blood' AND LAB.CATEGORY = 'Blood Gas' OR LAB.CATEGORY = 'Blood Gas'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses.long_title', 'like', '%history of tobacco use%'], ['lab.category', '=', 'blood'], ['lab.category', '=', 'blood gas or lab.category = blood gas']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "History of tobacco use" AND LAB."CATEGORY" = "Blood Gas"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'history of tobacco use'], ['lab."category"', '=', 'blood gas']]}

PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMITYEAR" < "2155" AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "ultrasound-heart"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admityear"', '<', '2155'], ['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'ultrasound-heart']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMITYEAR" < "2155" AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Dx ultrasound-heart"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admityear"', '<', '2155'], ['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'dx ultrasound-heart']]}
----------

how many patients with physical restrain status were treated with base drug?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "physical restrain status" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "base drug"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'physical restrain status'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'base drug']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Physical restrain status" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_TYPE" = "BASE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'physical restrain status'], ['prescriptions."drug_type"', '=', 'base']]}
----------

find the patients who have to be tested for ketones with diagnosis of dysphagia nos.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = 'dysphagia nos' AND LAB."LABEL" = 'ketones'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'dysphagia nos'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'ketones']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Dysphagia NOS" AND LAB."LABEL" = "Ketone"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'dysphagia nos'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'ketone']]}

----------
PREDS:
SELECT PROCEDURES."ICD9_CODE",PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" FROM PROCEDURES INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on PROCEDURES.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE PROCEDURES."SUBJECT_ID" = "74032"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['procedures."icd9_code"', 'prescriptions."drug"'], 'tab': ['procedures', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['procedures."subject_id"', '=', '74032']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE",PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_TYPE" FROM DIAGNOSES INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SUBJECT_ID" = "74032"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['diagnoses."short_title"', 'prescriptions."drug_type"'], 'tab': ['diagnoses', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."subject_id"', '=', '74032']]}

----------
PREDS:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."days_stay"', '>', '23'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenylephrine hcl']]}

among patients treated with base drug, how many of them had ventricular tachycardia as primary disease?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "VENTRICULAR TACHYCARDIA" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "base drug"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'ventricular tachycardia'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'base drug']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "VENTRICULAR TACHYCARDIA" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_TYPE" = "BASE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'ventricular tachycardia'], ['prescriptions."drug_type"', '=', 'base']]}

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'abdominal abscess'], ['prescriptions."drug_type"', '=', 'main']]}

how many of the patients with bradycardia had abnormal lab test results?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID") 
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC 
INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID 
INNER JOIN LAB ON DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID 
WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = 'bradycardia' 
AND LAB."FLAG" = 'abnormal'
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'lab'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'bradycardia'], ['lab."flag"', '=', 'abnormal']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "BRADYCARDIA" AND LAB."FLAG" = "abnormal"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'bradycardia'], ['lab."flag"', '=', 'abnormal']]}
----------

give me the number of emergency hospital admission patients who had insertion of 2 vascular stents.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" )
FROM DEMOGRAPHIC
INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_LOCATION" = "EMERGENCY HOSPITAL ADMISSION"
AND PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" = "Insertion of vascular stents"
GROUP BY DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID"
HAVING COUNT(PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE") = 2
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_location"', '=', 'emergency hospital admission'], ['procedures."long_title"', '=', 'insertion of vascular stents group by demographic. subject_id having count(procedures. long_title ) = 2']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Insert 2 vascular stents"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['procedures."short_title"', '=', 'insert 2 vascular stents']]}

----------

----------
PREDS:

INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID
INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID
WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."INSURANCE" = 'government health insurance'

AND PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = 'joint fluid lab test'

----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" != "0" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" != "1" AND DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "29570"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '!=', '0'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '!=', '1'], ['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', '29570']]}

TRUTH:

----------

FROM DEMOGRAPHIC
WHERE (DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_LOCATION" = 'admitted' OR DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_LOCATION" = 'transfer from hosp/extram')
AND (DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = 'dead' OR DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = 'expired on discharge')
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['(demographic."admission_location"', '=', 'admitted or demographic. admission_location = transfer from hosp/extram )'], ['(demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'dead or demographic. diagnosis = expired on discharge )']]}

SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMITYEAR" < "2173" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Ac salpingo-oophoritis"

what is the minimum days for which patients with brain mass; intracranial hemorrhage were hospitalized?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT MIN ( DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES D1 ON DEMOGRAPHIC."HADM_ID" = D1."HADM_ID" INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES D2 ON DEMOGRAPHIC."HADM_ID" = D2."HADM_ID" WHERE D1."LONG_TITLE" = 'brain mass' AND D2."LONG_TITLE" = 'intracranial hemorrhage'
{'sel': 'min', 'agg': ['demographic."days_stay"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['d1."long_title"', '=', 'brain mass'], ['d2."long_title"', '=', 'intracranial hemorrhage']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT MIN ( DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "BRAIN MASS;INTRACRANIAL HEMORRHAGE"
{'sel': 'min', 'agg': ['demographic."days_stay"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'brain mass;intracranial hemorrhage']]}

what is the maximum number of days of hospital stay for patients with hypertension but not coronary artery disease/cardiac cath?
----------
